The Gazette of the Democratic Socialist Republic of Sri Lanka

(Published by Authority)PART I : SECTION (I) — GENERALGovernment Notifications

1A - G 45125 - 53 (08/2025) This Gazette Extraordinary can be downloaded from www.documents.gov.lk

POST OFFICE ORDINANCE

RULE made by the Minister under Section 16 of the Post Office Ordinance (Chapter 190).

Dr. Nalinda Jayatissa, Minister of Health and Mass Media.

At Colombo,18th June, 2025.

Rule

Made under Section 16 of the Post Office Ordinance (Chapter 190) Published in Gazette No. 14707 of 5th August, 1966 as last amended by rule published in Gazette Extraordinary No. 1471/13 of 14th November, 2006 of the Democratic Socialist Republic of Sri Lanka is hereby further amended in the Schedule thereto, by the insertion after item 1166 of the following new items.

Year - 2005

1167. Postage and Revenue (Definitive) stamp issued under catalog number CSL-1560 on 14th December 2004, with a denomination of Rs. 5.00, similar in design to the stamp catalogued as CSL-1513, initially issued on 21 October 2003, with a denomination of Rs. 4.50, depicting a blue sapphire on a white and light blue background with mentioning “Blue Sapphire” and “Sri Lanka” in trilingual.

1168. Postage and Revenue (Commemorative) stamp issued under catalog number CSL-1561 on 13th March 2005, with a denomination of Rs. 5.00, featuring an upper body image of the Most Venerable Thalalle Siri Dhammananda Maha Nayaka Thero in a pale yellow colour background with mentioning “Sri Lanka’’ and “Most Venerable Thalalle Siri Dhammananda Maha Nayaka Thero’’in trilingual.

1169. Postage and Revenue (Definitive) stamp issued under catalog number CSL-1562 on 21st March 2005, surcharged with a denomination of 50 cents by striking out the original value with a black rectangular overlay, originally depicted an image of a Ceylon Blue Magpie on a branch and was issued on 13th December 1979 under the catalog number CSL-575.

1170. Postage and Revenue (Definitive) stamp issued under catalog number CSL-1563 on 21st March 2005, surcharged with a denomination of 50 cents by striking out the original value with a black rectangular overlay, originally depicted an image of a mangrove swamp and water and was issued on 11th November 1986 under the catalog number CSL-838.

1171. Postage and Revenue (Definitive) stamp issued under catalog number CSL-1564 on 21st March 2005, surcharged with a denomination of 50 cents by striking out the original value with a black rectangular overlay, originally depicted an image of a Fiddler Crab and mangroves in background and was issued on 11th November 1986 under the catalog number CSL-841.

1172. Postage and Revenue (Definitive) stamp issued under catalog number CSL-1565 on 21st March 2005, surcharged with a denomination of 50 cents by striking out the original value with a black rectangular overlay, originally depicted an image of the Lunar surface and earth from the moon and dark sky in the background and was issued on 10th November 1989 under the catalog number CSL-959.

1173. Postage and Revenue (Commemorative) stamp issued under catalog number CSL-1566 on 22nd March 2005, with a denomination of Rs. 5.00, depicting an upper body image of Most Venerable Professor Hammalawa Saddhathissa Nayaka Maha Thero in brown and gold colour background with mentioning “Sri Lanka’’ and “Most Ven. Prof. Hammalawa Saddhathissa Nayaka Maha Thero in trilingual.

1174. Postage and Revenue (Commemorative) stamp issued under catalog number CSL-1567 on 25th March 2005, with a denomination of Rs. 5.00, depicting an upper body image of Mr. T.B.Tennakoon in light brown colour background with mentioning “Sri Lanka’’ and “T.B.Tennakoon’’ in trilingual.

1175. Postage and Revenue (Commemorative) stamp issued under catalog number CSL-1568 on 30th March 2005, with a denomination of Rs. 5.00, depicting an upper-body image of Mr. D.A. Rajapaksa, a pagoda, a pitcher of plenty, the numbers “1905-2005” with beams spreading in six directions, a blue horizontal water current in the background of the pitcher of plenty, set against a light and dark purple background, with mentioning “Sri Lanka” and “D.A. Rajapaksa Birth Centenary” in trilingual.

1176. Postage and Revenue (Commemorative) stamp issued under catalog number CSL-1569 on 12th May 2005, with a denomination of Rs. 4.50, depicting an image of the fourth group performing ambulatory meditation, a world map in the background, a Buddhist flag, a withered Bo leaf, a fan with a Dharmachakra, and mentioning “Vesak” and “Sri Lanka” in trilingual, “Ambulatory Meditation” in English, and the dates “2549 B.E.” and “2005 A.D.”.

1177. Postage and Revenue (Commemorative) stamp issued under catalog number CSL-1570 on 12th May 2005, with a denomination of Rs. 5.00, depicting a Buddhist Thero delivering a Dhamma sermon to listeners, a world map in the background, a Buddhist flag, three Bo leaves in pink, green, and withered colours, a fan with a Dharmachakra, an image of the New York Buddhist Temple, and an oval-shaped logo centered on the Dharmachakra using six colours, with mentioning “Vesak” and “Sri Lanka” in trilingual, “Spiritual Bliss through Buddhism” and “NEW YORK BUDDHIST VIHARA - USA” in English, and the dates “2549 BE” and “2005 AD.”
